## LOG scalar: the separately retained projected result

**Derivative notation.** This once-projected source reference retains Gold's full covariant derivative. Step 6 transports it to the full project $`\mathcal D_a`$ and $`\Box^c`$, with Q/S/K connections. The [current certificate](../CERTIFICATION_LOG_TWO_FERMI_2026-09-25.md) establishes the retained-order project-frame result; no connection-only derivative replaces it.

The active calculation is the **unprojected superfield** in [STEP_04_LOG_SCALAR.md](../STEP_04_LOG_SCALAR.md), at the same stage as the primary and spinor. this separate file retains the earlier once-projected scalar result. It is regenerated from the same polynomial, with the C24 projection applied exactly once. The two expressions describe different stages; this file does not replace the active superfield result or perform the Gold-to-project convention conversion.

## 1. Source conventions and the projection

The [source conventions](../CONVENTIONS.md) and [working convention ledger](../LOG_CONVENTIONS.md) apply. Sources: C24 (2.15)–(2.16), [Components of curvature-squared invariants of minimal supergravity in five dimensions — PDF page 12](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.00679); supplement (1.2) and the following curvature identification, [Components of curvature-squared invariants of minimal supergravity in five dimensions — PDF page 103](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.00679). The full pre-projection derivation, including the trace sign, is in the active scalar document §§1–5.

<!-- ASSERT_CONVENTION: source_frame=Gold traceless Standard-Weyl components, metric_signature=(-,+,+,+,+), natural_units=hbar=c=1, fermions=all degrees retained, component_projection=exactly once, Gold_to_project_conversion=not applied -->

We retain the five-dimensional Gold signature $`(-,+,+,+,+)`$, Clifford relation $`\{\Gamma^a,\Gamma^b\}=-2\eta^{ab}`$, $`a=0,\ldots,4`$, $`\alpha=1,\ldots,4`$, $`i=1,2`$, and the original Grassmann order. No EOM, gauge fixing, auxiliary elimination, integration by parts or fermion truncation is used. Write $`U|:=U(x,\theta)|_{\theta=0}`$. C24 gives

```math
\chi_\alpha^i=\frac{3i}{32}X_\alpha^i|,\qquad D=-\frac{3}{128}Y|,\qquad
\Phi_{ab}^{ij}=-\frac{3i}{4}X_{ab}^{ij}|.
\tag{S4-P-01}
```

Solving each constant rescaling, using $`1/i=-i`$, gives

```math
X_\alpha^i|=\frac{32}{3i}\chi_\alpha^i=-\frac{32i}{3}\chi_\alpha^i,\qquad
Y|=-\frac{128}{3}D,\qquad
X_{ab}^{ij}|=-\frac{4}{3i}\Phi_{ab}^{ij}=\frac{4i}{3}\Phi_{ab}^{ij}.
\tag{S4-P-02}
```

The spinor-tensor version has the same constant factor, $`X_{\alpha\beta}^{ij}|=(4i/3)\Phi_{\alpha\beta}^{ij}`$, because the two-form/spinor map uses constant gamma matrices. We write $`w_{ab}=W_{ab}|`$ and $`w_{\alpha\beta}=W_{\alpha\beta}|`$. The vector-multiplet components retain the source names $`W,\lambda_\alpha^i,X^{ij},F_{ab},F_{\alpha\beta}`$ after projection. The higher Weyl tensors and all geometric tensors carry explicit bars in the final expression, for example $`W_{\alpha\beta\gamma}{}^i|`$ and $`\mathcal R(M)_{a\alpha}{}^{ibc}|`$.

Projection is even, so it introduces no Grassmann interchange:

```math
(AB)|=(A|)(B|),\qquad
\left.c\,W^{-n}X_\alpha^iY\,U\right|
=c\left(-\frac{32i}{3}\right)\left(-\frac{128}{3}\right)W^{-n}\chi_\alpha^iD\,(U|)
=\frac{4096i}{9}c\,W^{-n}\chi_\alpha^iD\,(U|).
\tag{S4-P-03}
```

More generally, each written monomial coefficient is multiplied by

```math
\left(-\frac{32i}{3}\right)^{n_X}
\left(-\frac{128}{3}\right)^{n_Y}
\left(\frac{4i}{3}\right)^{n_Z},
\tag{S4-P-04}
```

where $`n_X`$ counts Weyl spinors $`X_\alpha^i`$, $`n_Y`$ counts $`Y`$, and $`n_Z`$ counts triplet Weyl tensors $`X_{ab}^{ij}`$ or $`X_{\alpha\beta}^{ij}`$, including their vector derivatives. The vector auxiliary $`X^{ij}`$ is not counted in $`n_X`$ or $`n_Z`$. Ordered factors and index contractions are retained term by term.

Here $`\nabla_a`$ means the **full projected covariant derivative** of C24 (2.16). Thus $`(\nabla_aU)|=\nabla_a(U|)`$ in the induced component representation, with its Q, S and K connections included. For example,

```math
(\nabla_aX_\alpha^i)|=-\frac{32i}{3}\nabla_a\chi_\alpha^i,\qquad
(\nabla_a\nabla_bY)|=-\frac{128}{3}\nabla_a\nabla_bD.
\tag{S4-P-05}
```

No replacement by a Levi–Civita or partial derivative is made. Expanding these connections into ordinary component fields is a separate operation.

## 2. Scalar-sector checks and scope

The independently derived algebraic terms map as follows:

```math
\left.\frac{69}{1024}W^{ab}W_{ab}Y\right|
=\frac{69}{1024}\left(-\frac{128}{3}\right)w^{ab}w_{ab}D
=-\frac{23}{8}w^{ab}w_{ab}D,\qquad
\left.\frac{3}{2048}Y^2\right|
=\frac{3}{2048}\frac{16384}{9}D^2=\frac83D^2.
\tag{S4-P-06}
```

The earlier comparison to printed C24 coefficients remains in [note 05](../notes/05_two_bosonic_corrections_source_audit.md). These replacements are applied once in this reference file and are not fed back into the active superfield calculation.

All 58 primary contributions and all 66,386 written scalar summands are retained. This is an unreduced count, not a count of independent tensor monomials. For each summand, the exact rational/imaginary projection factor is checked against its inverse; weights, parity and index contractions remain those of the parent scalar. The projected expression has Weyl weight four. Full-primary defining-projector equality and curved S/K/linearity now pass. Project-frame projection, canonicalization, reality, current conservation and BF construction are certified through the retained order in [the current certificate](../CERTIFICATION_LOG_TWO_FERMI_2026-09-25.md). The conservation and supersymmetry conclusions use verified multiplet premises and the complete superform proof; a separate brute-force variation of all expanded terms is not claimed. Equality to the complete literal all-fermion C24 basis and an unrestricted expanded variation are not claimed.
